Conclusion: 集体级别的信号对于合成数据管理是必要的，研究为模型开发者提供了一种廉价的诊断工具，可以在有限的计算预算中进行有效的数据优先级评估。

Abstract: Synthetic data now is essential to LLM training, used to strengthen advanced capabilities such as autonomous and long-horizon task execution. Yet recent work shows that training on it at scale can degrade model generation, making it important to decide what synthetic data is worth training on. While current data curation practices do so with individual-level signals (i.e., estimates of each data sample's training utility in isolation), across pre-training and post-training settings we show that this is insufficient for synthetic data, and that group-level signals (i.e., estimates of utility that account for interactions among data samples) are necessary for effective data curation. First, we show that individual-level signals are blind to how samples jointly affect training: synthetic datasets with different compositions can be indistinguishable under individual-level influence yet differ sharply under group-level influence, and curating by the latter yields better downstream performance, particularly in generative capability. Second, we find that group-level signals matter more as training pipelines become increasingly synthetic: among widely used data curation methods, only those incorporating them improve over baseline, with gains increasing when weights capturing relations among samples are amplified. Finally, we translate these findings into practice -- for model developers under a compute budget, we offer a cheap diagnostic that prioritizes which groups of synthetic data most need group-level estimation, recovering much of the benefit of full group-level scoring at a fraction of the compute cost.

</details>


### [111] [Can large language models unlock discrete data in ophthalmic diagnostic reports?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00795)
*Umair A. Zaidi,An-Lun Wu,Wei-Chun Lin,Thomas S. Hwang,Michelle R. Hribar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 利用两种提示策略，大型语言模型评估了从眼科诊断PDF报告中提取结构化数据的准确性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过比较两种提示策略，验证大型语言模型在眼科诊断PDF报告结构化数据提取中的应用，以提高数据提取的准确性与效率。

Method: 使用了两种不同的大规模语言模型处理流程：一种是Schema-Constrained方法，定义了预设的JSON模式；另一种是Prompt-Only方法，仅提供了详细的指令提示并转换为JSON格式。

Result: Schema-Constrained方法的值准确率在Visual Field和RNFL Single Exam报告中为100%，在Glaucoma Overview和Thickness Map报告中分别为97.45%和98.00%；而Prompt-Only方法所有报告类型的值准确率为100%。所有报告类型的Schema-Constrained方法和全部报告类型的100%的格式准确率；除了RNFL Single Exam报告外，Prompt-Only方法的格式准确率为100%。手工审查的平均提取时间为每份报告56.51秒，而Schema-Constrained方法和Prompt-Only方法分别为每报告5.04秒和4.70秒，减少了大约92%的时间。

Conclusion: 该小型的概念验证数据集表明，通用目的语言模型辅助管道可以从眼科诊断PDF中高效、准确地提取结构化数据。Prompt-Only方法具有最高的值准确度，而Schema-Constrained方法提供了一致的模式输出并保持了100%的格式准确率。这两种方法的互补优势支持了进一步对混合验证工作流的研究与临床数据提取的评估。

Abstract: Objective: To assess the accuracy and efficiency of a large language model (LLM) using two prompt strategies to extract structured data from ophthalmic diagnostic PDF reports. Methods: Twenty deidentified reports across four types (Visual Field, OCT Glaucoma Overview, OCT retinal nerve fiber layer Single Exam, and OCT Thickness Map; n = 5 each) were processed using two GPT-4o-assisted pipelines and compared with a reconciled manual ground truth. Schema-Constrained used Structured Output mode with a predefined JSON Schema; Prompt-Only used a detailed instruction prompt followed by Python conversion to JSON. Outcomes were value accuracy, formatting accuracy, and extraction time. Results: Schema-Constrained value accuracy was 100.00% for Visual Field and RNFL Single Exam, 97.45% for Glaucoma Overview, and 98.00% for Thickness Map; Prompt-Only achieved 100.00% across all four report types. Formatting accuracy was 100.00% for Schema-Constrained across all report types and 100.00% for Prompt-Only except RNFL Single Exam (90.14%). Mean extraction time was 56.51 s per report for manual review versus 5.04 s for Schema-Constrained and 4.70 s for Prompt-Only, an approximately 92% reduction. Conclusions: In this small proof-of-concept dataset, general-purpose LLM-assisted pipelines extracted structured data from ophthalmic diagnostic PDFs with high accuracy and substantially reduced processing time. Prompt-Only achieved the highest value accuracy, while Schema-Constrained produced schema-compliant output with 100% formatting accuracy. These complementary strengths support further evaluation of hybrid, validation-aware workflows for research and clinical data abstraction.

</details>


### [112] [Verbalized and Internal Probabilities Are Coupled in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00827)
*Sinead Williamson,Jiaxuan Li,Nick Foti,Russ Webb,Masha Fedzechkina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过系统地干预数据中的不确定来源，发现LLMs的内部概率读数和口头概率读数均受训练数据中分布性和断言性不确定的影响，且这两种概率读数之间的对齐程度超出了各自独立追踪同一不确定来源的预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的工作已经表明内部概率和口头概率可以分别追踪训练数据中的频率和断言概率，但不清楚这两种读数是否一致。本文通过深度探究LLMs在训练和编码时对不确定性的处理方式填补了这一空白。

Method: 研究通过对训练数据中的不确定来源进行干预来系统地考察LLMs的响应变化。

Result: 研究发现LLMs的内部概率读数和口头概率读数均受到训练数据中分布性和断言性不确定的影响。同时，研究还发现这两种概率读数之间的对齐程度超出了独立追踪同一不确定来源的预期。

Conclusion: 这表明口头概率可以用来探究模型的内部概率分布，从而可以使用口头概率作为内部概率分布的代理指标。

Abstract: Large language models carry an internal notion of uncertainty in their sampling distribution, i.e., the probabilities they place on generating one answer rather than another. They can also be asked to state a confidence, in words or as a number: a verbalized uncertainty. Prior work suggests that internal probabilities track relative frequencies in the training data, and that verbalized probabilities track explicit probabilistic assertions in the training data. However, we do not know whether these two readouts are aligned, except when frequencies and probabilistic assertions in the training data happen to align. This limits our understanding of when we can use verbalized uncertainties as a proxy for either training data frequencies, or a model's internal distribution. We resolve this gap by systematically exploring how LLMs probability readouts are impacted by training and in-context data, via intervening on the underlying uncertainty sources in the data. We find that both internal and verbalized probability readouts are impacted by both distributional and asserted uncertainty in the training data. Further, we find that verbalized and internal probabilities are aligned beyond what would be expected by independently tracking the same uncertainty sources, suggesting that verbalized probabilities can be used to probe a model's internal distribution.

</details>


### [113] [VERITYGATE: A Four-Gate Schema-Level Faithfulness Framework and Paired Benchmark for Grounded LLM Narrations over Structured Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00833)
*Sachin Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VERITYGATE 是一个用于检查生成的声明是否符合预定义框架的四关卡验证器。验证结果显示，使用该工具验证后，大多数模型生成的声明在准确性上有所提升，但增幅有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型生成的解释不总是与严密的系统证据相一致，为了解决这一问题，作者开发了VERITYGATE这个验证框架，并设置了若干关卡来检查生成的内容是否符合预定义规则。

Method: VERITYGATE 是基于一个固定框架（四关卡）来检查生成内容符合预设规则，而不是详细的内容校验。该实验方法包括 GPT-4o-mini、Llama-3.3-70B 和 Claude Sonnet 4.6 的实验，并进行了两次校正。

Result: 通过VERITYGATE验证前，迷你模型的准确性为80.3%，Sonnet模型为47.9%；校正后，迷你模型微增至28.0%，Sonnet模型增至54.3%；此外，Sonnet模型经过第二次验证后，验证通过的声明相比第一次并无明显提升。

Conclusion: 尽管验证工具提高了模型生成的准确性，但增加的幅度不大，且在某些情况下，验证工具并不能完全保证生成内容的正确性。

Abstract: Fluent LLM explanations may not follow the evidence from a structured system. We present VERITYGATE, a four-gate checker for declared evidence IDs, entities, numbers, and claim types. It checks a fixed schema; it does not verify every fact in the prose. At r=0 and r=1, we test 900 instances per setting (450 grounded-ungrounded pairs) with GPT-4o-mini, Llama-3.3-70B, and Claude Sonnet 4.6. Under this schema-level contract and before repair, 80.3% of mini claims and 47.9% of Sonnet claims fail. These are verifier rejection rates, not prose-hallucination rates. One repair pass raises claim survival from 19.7% to 28.0% for mini and from 52.1% to 54.3% for Sonnet. Verified claims per example change by +0.14 for mini, -0.71 for Llama, and -0.47 for Sonnet, so survival and output volume must be reported together. A second Sonnet pass gives no clear gain. At r=1, Gate 4 covers 97.0%, 98.7%, and 100% of failing claims for mini, Llama, and Sonnet. Small human studies support the rules but show gaps between schema checks and correct prose. A domain-specific GPT-4o judge test shows an order effect, so it is only a usefulness check. We release the code and data.

</details>


### [114] [Contextual trajectory and incremental contextual displacement: Towards using LLMs to understand dynamic, utterance-specific meaning construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00840)
*Grayson Wycliffe Storer,Julia Witte Zimmerman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过使用基于变压器的大型语言模型（如RoBERTa）在不同文本上下文中重新计算词嵌入，构建了每个词的增量轨迹，从而研究了句子特定意义的构建，并发现这种信息不仅存在于句子级的代表中，也存在于普通词汇标记中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨基于Transformer的大型语言模型在处理具有歧义的句子时，如何通过上下文信息生成和转换词嵌入，进而解释句子特有的意义构建。

Method: 利用garden-path句子作为测试案例，通过反复重新计算随着句子扩展而添加的词对每个词的词嵌入重新计算来构建增量轨迹，并引入了量化表示位移的多种度量标准。

Result: 增量轨迹能够重现garden-path处理已知特征，包括中断发生在关键区域，可靠地区分garden-path句子与匹配的消歧控制。发现与歧义相关的信息既可以从句子级别的CLS表示中获取，也可以从普通词汇标记中获取，表明句子级信息分布在多个表示尺度上。

Conclusion: 增量轨迹为使用大型语言模型研究句子特定意义构建提供了一个有前途的框架，结果表明歧义相关的信息不仅存在于句子级别的表示中，也存在于普通的词标记中，这表明句义信息在多个表示尺度上被分布。这些发现揭示了其他歧义和误导相关的语言现象中的相似轨迹结构，进一步验证了增量轨迹的普适性。

Abstract: Transformer-based large language models (LLMs) such as RoBERTa represent text using contextual word embeddings (CWEs), which alter the embeddings associated with each token based on surrounding context. We construct token-wise incremental trajectories by repeatedly recomputing a token's CWE as successive words are added to a sentence, yielding a representation of how contextualized embeddings evolve as the utterance unfolds. We evaluate this approach using garden-path sentences as a test case with characteristic features. Token-wise trajectories reproduce known features of garden-path processing, including disruption around the critical region, and reliably distinguish garden-path sentences from matched disambiguated controls. We introduce several metrics for quantifying representational displacement across contextual increments and show that trajectory information can be highly predictive of sentence type. We find that ambiguity-related information is recoverable not only from the sentence-level CLS representation but also from ordinary vocabulary tokens, suggesting that utterance-level information is distributed across multiple representational scales. In exploratory analyses, we find qualitatively similar trajectory structures in other ambiguity- and misdirection-related linguistic phenomena. Together, these results establish token-wise incremental trajectories as a promising framework for studying utterance-specific meaning construction using LLMs.

</details>


### [115] [Child-Adapted Structured Phonological Representations for Interpretable Speech Sound Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00852)
*Abner Hernandez,Tomás Arias Vergara,Andreas Maier,Paula Andrea Pérez-Toro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过调整PhonoQ-2.0模型以适应儿童语音，结果显示儿童语音的适应性在所有监督条件下改善了音位的识别，特别是对于浊音的识别。同时揭示了特定音位的变化与临床进展的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前音位学表示模型主要针对成人语音进行训练，但儿童语音具有不同的音位特征。为了提供更准确的儿童语音音位学表示，该研究使用CHILDES-Aligned数据集对PhonoQ-2.0模型进行儿童语音的适应性调整，通过比较不同监督条件和初始化策略的效果，评估其在儿童语音中的泛化能力。

Method: 研究通过PhonoQ-2.0模型对儿童语音进行适配，采用成人语音和儿童语音的数据进行对比，同时使用两种初始化策略进行模型训练。对适配后的模型进行宏平均F1分数评估，分析不同音位变化的监督条件对模型性能的影响，并通过纵向超音位学分析进一步评估模型的稳定性和一致性。

Result: 在58名一般发展儿童的1352个辅音目标中，适应儿童语音后，浊音识别率显著提升，从成人PhonoQ的0.922提升至0.972-0.987。而对于气息音的识别，适配模型表现出较好的稳健性，表现为0.871-0.902的得分，尽管不同模型在各个子类的表现存在较大差异。进一步的纵向超音位学分析显示，所有的模型都保持了儿童的颚前音和后牙龈音的变化趋势，这些变化与报告的临床进展一致。

Conclusion: 该研究通过调整PhonoQ-2.0模型以适应儿童语音，成功提升了模型在识别儿童语音上的准确性，尤其是浊音和气息音的识别。这为进一步研究儿童语音提供了有力的音位学表示方法。

Abstract: Structured phonological representations provide an interpretable alternative to generic speech embeddings, but existing models are largely trained on adult speech. We adapt PhonoQ-2.0 to child speech using CHILDES-Aligned data and compare three alignment-supervision conditions (Adult, Adult+Child, and Child-only) across two initialization strategies (Adult PhonoQ and scratch). Generalization is evaluated against manual child-speech annotations. On 1,352 consonant targets from 58 typically developing children, child-speech adaptation improves voicing recognition across all supervision conditions, from 0.922 macro-F1 for Adult PhonoQ to 0.972--0.987 after adaptation. Manner is more sensitive to alignment supervision: Adult+Child MFA reaches 0.804 and 0.796, compared to approximately 0.70 under Adult MFA supervision. Place remains comparatively strong across systems (0.871--0.902), although per-class performance varies substantially. The velar-fronting contrast is preserved across all seven model variants. Longitudinal UltraPhonix analysis further reveals speaker-specific velar and post-alveolar changes that are largely preserved across models and broadly consistent with reported clinical progress.

</details>


### [116] [DeBERTa-ConPara: Attack-Aware and Deployment-Realistic Detection of AI-Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00883)
*Mohamed Mady,Yupei Li,Johannes Reschke,Björn W. Schuller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DeBERTa-ConPara 是一种新型的部署导向型 AI 文本检测器，通过结合攻击感知的 Unicode 预处理和特定于域的训练，该模型在多种攻击中的检测性能显著提升，特别是在 homoglyph 和 zero-width-space 插入攻击上的表现尤为出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对实际部署环境中，AI 生成文本检测的挑战性，文章提出了一种新型的 DeBERTa-ConPara 检测器，旨在解决分布偏移、对抗性扰动、以及缺乏目标域标签带来的检测器性能下降问题。

Method: 文章中，作者提出了基于 HC3 Plus、M4、MAGE 和 RAID 数据集进行训练的 DeBERTa-ConPara 模型，并通过攻击感知的 Unicode 预处理方法优化了模型性能。特别是在训练数据预处理和推理阶段进行不同的预处理，以增强模型对不同类型攻击的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验结果表明，DeBERTa-ConPara 模型在 RAID 隐藏测试数据集上达到了 99.61% 的 AUROC，99.01% 的 TPR@5% FPR 和 96.57% 的 TPR@1% FPR。同时，这种方法在不同的攻击类别中，尤其是在 homoglyph 和 zero-width-space 插入攻击上的效果尤为显著，准确率分别从 11.05% 和 1.12% 提升至 96.98%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过将攻击感知的预处理与特定于域的训练相结合，可以在实际部署环境中改善 AI 文本检测器的性能。而通过实验证明，自然语言转换和监督对比学习方法在提高模型检测性能方面效果有限，甚至可能产生负面影响。

Abstract: Robust detection of AI-generated text under deployment conditions is challenging: distribution shifts across domains and generators, adversarial perturbations of the input surface, and the absence of target-domain labels for threshold calibration all degrade detectors that perform well in-domain. We present DeBERTa-ConPara, a deployment-oriented detector combining attack-aware Unicode preprocessing with a contextual transformer encoder trained over HC3 Plus, M4, MAGE and RAID. Our central finding is that preprocessing acts in opposite directions depending on where it is applied: normalising the training corpus deduplicates it, collapsing 35.4% of RAID rows into copies of their clean siblings and deleting the adversarial supervision, whereas normalising at inference is an effective defence. A factorial varying the two placements independently identifies raw training with normalised inference as the best configuration, reaching 99.61% AUROC, 99.01% TPR@5% FPR and 96.57% TPR@1% FPR on the official RAID hidden test, alongside 93.14% average balanced accuracy across HC3 Plus and MAGE under a fixed threshold. The gain is confined to two of twelve attack classes: homoglyph and zero-width-space insertion rise from 11.05% and 1.12% to 96.98%. The same signature reproduces in a zero-shot detector of different architecture, showing the effect belongs to the attacks rather than to our model. We additionally report two negative results: semantic-invariance augmentation through paraphrasing and supervised contrastive learning (ConPara) does not improve the best configuration, and the handcrafted feature-fusion branch is inert in distribution and harmful outside it.

</details>


### [117] [The Geometry of Contextual Relations: Language Models Address Facts by Order of Mention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00910)
*Yufa Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究通过分析语言模型在不同类型问题上的隐藏状态变化，提出了一个称为'序数地址假设'的模型组织理论，发现语言模型在处理事实时依赖事实提到的顺序，而非事实本身的名称，这种依赖有序性、可控性、低秩性和自发生长的特性揭示了语言模型的理解机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索语言模型在处理问题时如何利用其内部表示来快速访问相关的上下文信息，特别是当问题涉及多个已给出的背景事实时。

Method: 研究人员通过向语言模型提供一组相关事实，然后问关于这些事实中各个实体的问题，观察模型的隐藏状态变化。特别地，他们测量了当问题从一个实体转向另一个实体时，模型隐藏状态的变化，这种变化被称为'序数向量'。通过对比不同语言模型（Qwen、Gemma、Llama），验证了这些发现并探讨了这些特征的普遍性。

Result: 实验结果揭示了语言模型在处理问题时表现出的四个关键特性：事实的提到顺序决定了模型如何组织其内部表示，模型能够通过应用序数向量来精确定位到所提出问题对应的响应；这种组织方式是可控的，能够精确地访问到不同的事实；事实地址存在于模型中的一个低秩子空间中，类似于人类的记忆机制；这些特性是自然地在模型的训练过程中形成的，并且在不同的模型中普遍存在。

Conclusion: 研究提出并验证了序数地址假设，这一假设深化了我们对语言模型理解机制的理解，并可能对未来模型设计和改进产生重要影响。

Abstract: Human reasoning depends on how objects are related within propositions. \textit{How do relations organize the language representations of contextual contents?} We give an LLM a list of facts in its context (e.g., \emph{Alice eats an apple. Bob eats a pear.}) and measure how its hidden state changes when the question switches from what Alice eats to what Bob eats. Averaged over many lists, this change is a steering vector, which we call the \emph{ordinal vector}. It points to a fact by its \emph{order of mention}, the order in which the facts were stated in the context. We find that LLMs represent the fact a question asks about by its order of mention, not by the name the question contains. We state this as the \textit{ordinal addressing hypothesis}: each order of mention has a \emph{fact address} in the model's state, shared by all contexts, and a question moves the state to the fact address of the fact it asks about, while the context supplies what that fact says. Across Qwen, Gemma, and Llama, fact addresses are (1) \emph{ordered by mention}: query states are organized by the order of facts, not of names, even when one fact has multiple subjects; (2) \emph{steerable}: added to a question about the first fact of a new list, the ordinal vector makes the model answer with the second fact of that list; (3) \emph{low-rank}: they span a low-rank subspace in which the first-mentioned fact is the easiest to reach, surprisingly similar to human recall; and (4) \emph{emergent}: they are shared in late-middle layers, hold from 1.5B to 32B parameters, and form early in pretraining. Language models reach a stated fact by where it was mentioned, deepening our understanding of LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [118] [Efficient Task Adaptation in Large Language Models: A Survey of Weight-Based, Prompt-Based, and Embedding-Based Adaptations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00928)
*Jungwon Park,Changin Choi,Jimyeong Kim,Nojun Kwak,Wonjong Rhee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文总结了一个统一框架，通过任务信息编码的位置和方式来分类任务适应方法，探讨了不同适应方法的优缺点及其关系，并揭示了未来研究的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于大型语言模型被广泛应用于各种下游任务，因此如何高效地进行任务适应成为了一个核心挑战。目前，针对此问题已经提出了多种方法，但这些方法大多在各自的框架内演化，缺乏跨框架的研究和比较。

Method: 本文首先定义了一个统一的框架，明确区分了任务信息在模型权重、输入提示或嵌入式任务信息中的编码位置。其次，通过一个全面的分类图来整合这些框架，并分析每种方法的优势和劣势，以此解释不同适应框架的发展脉络，澄清了不同框架之间的关系，指出了未来研究中存在的开放性问题。

Result: 文本通过提供一个整合不同适应方法的框架，澄清了不同方法之间的关系，并分析了每种方法的优缺点，有助于理解和指导未来的研究。

Conclusion: 该调查为未来的研究提供了有价值的见解，并为任务适应方法的未来发展方向提供了指导。

Abstract: As large language models are increasingly deployed across diverse downstream tasks, efficient task adaptation has emerged as a central challenge. In response, a wide range of task adaptation methods have been proposed, spanning parameter-efficient fine-tuning, in-context learning, and embedding-injection approaches. However, these lines of work have largely evolved within individual paradigms, leaving their cross-paradigm relationships and trade-offs underexplored, especially for recently emerging embedding-based adaptations. This survey presents a unified framework that categorizes task adaptation methods by where and how task information is encoded: model weights, input prompts, or injected task embeddings. We provide a comprehensive taxonomy that integrates these paradigms, analyze their key strengths and limitations to explain how different adaptation paradigms have evolved, clarify relationships across paradigms, and highlight open problems for future research.

</details>


### [119] [ReHoPER: Receding-Horizon Planning for Enhanced Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00940)
*Saeed Ahmadnia,Cornelia Caragea*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReHoPER 是一种无需标注数据或任务特定提示设计的推理方法，它通过生成和回答多个中间问题来增强大规模语言模型的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的大规模语言模型在面对具有高度组合性的任务时表现不足，因此提出了 ReHoPER 方法来提升其推理能力。

Method: ReHoPER 方法通过迭代规划候选中间问题，选择一个回答，然后从更新的历史中重新规划，最终生成回答。

Result: 在 iLLC 等多个数据集上，ReHoPER 方法相较于强基线方法表现更好，特别是在具有高度组合性的设定中。

Conclusion: ReHoPER 方法展示了在无需特定任务标签或提示设计的情况下，仍能显著提升语言模型的推理性能。

Abstract: We propose ReHoPER, an inference-only, zero-shot method that improves large language models' reasoning by generating and answering intermediate questions along multiple paths before the final answer. It iteratively plans a horizon of candidate intermediate questions, selects one to answer, and replans from the updated history. ReHoPER is task-agnostic, using the same generic instructions across datasets and models without labeled data or task-specific prompt design. Across multiple datasets, including iLLC, a new controlled benchmark for compositional reasoning, ReHoPER outperforms strong baselines, with the largest gains in the most compositional settings. Our implementation and the iLLC generator are publicly available to support future work.

</details>


### [120] [Beyond Leaderboards: Tokenomics of Agentic Small Language Model Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00954)
*Alexei N. Skurikhin,Emily M. Taylor,Nathan A. DeBardeleben*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）从独立助手过渡到有执行力的工作流程时，评估标准应超越单一的准排行榜准确性，考虑操作可靠性、成本、延迟和令牌效率。通过一个基于小语言模型（SLMs）的案例研究，展示了团队协作和反馈机制在保持高性能的同时降低运营成本的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs的先进性增加，评估标准需要扩展，以适应新的运作需求。当前的单一评估方法只关注准确性，而忽略了其他关键因素，导致一些模型在实际应用中的表现较差。论文提出了一种新的评估方案，考虑了多维度的评估指标。

Method: 研究采用了一种基于SLMs的小模型集成方法，引入了小模型裁判和反馈循环机制。论文在IFEval基准测试中的541个提示下进行了测试，并将结果与最强的独立LLM模型进行比较以验证效果。

Result: 研究结果显示，在IFEval基准测试中，最优的SLMs团队达到了97.34%的严格指令准确率，比最强的独立LLM模型（gpt-5.4）高出5.81个百分点。团队以较低的成本运行，研究还进一步分析了其背后的经济和操作行为，如每样本成本、令牌组成、有用输出吞吐量、反馈循环恢复、延迟分解、不同指令类别和约束计数的性能等。

Conclusion: 研究确认了基于SLM的团队合作和反馈机制能提高指令执行的准确性，同时还能降低运营成本。这些结果表明，未来针对有执行力的AI系统的评估应考虑多层次的指标，而不仅仅是准确性。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) move from standalone assistants into agentic workflows, evaluation must extend beyond scalar leaderboard accuracy to account for operational reliability, cost, latency, and token efficiency. We use an agentic ensemble of small language models (SLMs) with an SLM-judge-mediated feedback loop as a case study for such beyond-leaderboard evaluation. On the 541-prompt IFEval benchmark, the best ensemble achieves 97.34% strict prompt accuracy, exceeding the strongest standalone LLM baseline, gpt-5.4, by 5.81 percentage points while operating in a lower-cost regime. We then analyze the tokenomics and operational behavior behind this gain, including cost per sample, token composition, useful-output goodput, feedback-loop recovery, latency decomposition, and performance across instruction categories and constraint counts. Our results show that agentic SLM ensembles can trade additional test-time tokens and orchestration overhead for improved instruction-following fidelity, motivating multi-dimensional evaluation protocols for future agentic AI systems.

</details>


### [121] [Role-aware Heuristic Episodic Attention for Conversational LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00958)
*Wanyang Hong,Zhaoning Zhang,Yi Chen,Libo Zhang,Baihui Liu,Linbo Qiao,Zhiliang Tian,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大语言模型在多轮对话中累积背景知识衰减的三种失败模式，并提出了REAI框架来管理对话背景，提高模型判断分数并减少延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在进行多轮对话时，往往很容易丢失持续的指令和相关的信息。文章通过REAI框架，提出了一种新的方法，以保持对话的连贯性和对指令的遵守。

Method: 研究通过分析注意力污染、稀释和漂移等三种失效模式，设计了REAI框架，区别对待指令记忆和情节记忆，采用角色感知的启发式检索机制管理历史信息。

Result: REAI框架在Long-MT-Bench+上的判分得分从6.32提高到7.36（10分制），相对增益达到16.5%，同时将平均延迟降低了2.91倍。此外，在多种语言角色扮演任务中也展现出改进效果。

Conclusion: 研究表明REAI框架能够在提高语言模型对话连续性和指令一致性的方面取得显著效果。

Abstract: Large language models often lose track of persistent instructions and relevant information as multi-turn conversations grow. We study this cumulative contextual decay through three related failure modes: attention pollution, dilution, and drift. We propose REA (Role-aware Heuristic Episodic Attention), a context-management framework that assigns different persistence and representation policies to instructions and episodic interactions. Instructional Memory retains identified global constraints in a dedicated prefix. Episodic Memory preserves user inputs and compresses model replies, while heuristic retrieval selects raw text, compressed representations, or omission for each historical turn. On Long-MT-Bench+, REA improves the judge score from 6.32 to 7.36 on a 10-point scale, a 16.5% relative gain over the Vanilla baseline, and reduces average latency by 2.91$\times$. Additional evaluations show aggregate gains on three backbones spanning 1.7B-7B parameters and on Chinese and English role-playing tasks. These results support role-aware context management as a practical approach to maintaining conversational continuity and instruction adherence.

</details>


### [122] [A Citation-Grounded Benchmark for Trustworthy Earnings Call Transcript Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00969)
*Yingzhu Zhao,Vlad Pandelea,Han Yuan,Bo Hu,Wuqiong Luo,Li Zhang,Zheng Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种无需专家标注的数字证据评估方法，用于大型语言模型（LLMs）的接地性评估，并构建了ECTs-100数据集来评估接地性和正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于现有的LSTM模型在财务文档分析中存在评估成本高、可扩展性差等问题，研究旨在通过自动化数据集构建管道和数字证据评估方法，解决现有技术的挑战并提供新的基准。

Method: 研究设计了一种基于数字证据的评估方法，通过分析LSTM模型生成的分析性语句，并收集了来自标准普尔500指数前100成分公司的收益电话记录（ECTs）作为数据集。

Result: 实验结果显示LSTM模型在接地性方面表现良好，但在正确性方面面临显著挑战，信息不足成为主要问题。

Conclusion: 研究建议进一步加强LSTM模型的 grounding 和 correct 性能，并提出了实用的“自觉的不熟练”模式来提醒模型当有足够的证据时不要产生未支持的幻觉。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been increasingly used for financial document analysis, including earnings call transcripts (ECTs). Beyond generating standalone claims, users increasingly prefer grounded analyses that pair claims with verifiable citations from source documents to enable independent validation. However, evaluating such analytical claims typically requires extensive expert annotation, which is costly and difficult to scale, and real-world financial analysis commonly involves long context-question-answer triplets, further increasing task complexity. To address these challenges and benchmark the current landscape of grounded analysis by LLMs, we propose a numeric evidence evaluation method that enables groundedness assessment without reliance on expert annotation. We also introduce an automated dataset construction pipeline and construct ECTs-100 from the top 100 constituents of the S&P 500 to support benchmark of both groundedness and correctness. In addition, we examine conscious incompetence, a practical failure mode in financial analysis in which LLMs must detect when available evidence is insufficient and refrain from producing unsupported hallucinations. Empirical results show that LLMs perform well in groundedness but face notable limitations in correctness, with informational insufficiency presenting an additional challenge.

</details>


### [123] [The Devil Is in the Reconstruction Loss Scale: Rethinking Optimization in LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00983)
*Chao Li,Shigeng Wang,Anbang Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文全面研究了顺序量化中的优化过程，揭示了重建损失和梯度不均衡的问题，通过使用不同级别的均方根误差（RMSE）来自然实现梯度规范化，进而改进基于学习的顺序量化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的顺序量化方法主要使用均方误差（MSE）作为重建损失函数，但重建损失和梯度的不均衡问题尚未得到充分理解。本文旨在全面探究优化过程，并提出改进建议。

Method: 通过广泛的实证研究，涉及多种学习基于顺序量化方法、LLM家族、模型规模、架构、量化参数和任务，揭示重建损失和梯度不均衡的问题。

Result: 发现基于MSE的顺序量化存在优化不均衡问题，且使用不同级别定义的均方根误差（RMSE）版本自然实现了梯度规范化，表现优于MSE。

Conclusion: 文章提出优化强度应与重建损失规模脱钩的原则，并提出了使用不同级别的RMSE进行优化改进的建议。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) methods typically use sequential quantization that partitions a pre-trained LLM into a series of units (e.g., transformer blocks), with one unit quantized at each stage. State-of-the-art PTQ methods are predominantly learning-based, optimizing auxiliary quantization parameters (e.g., scaling factors, rotation matrices, clipping thresholds, and adapters) via gradient descent to minimize a reconstruction loss. A common practice is to use mean squared error (MSE) as the reconstruction loss function, yet its induced optimization behavior remains largely unexplored. In this work, we take a holistic view of sequential quantization and systematically investigate how optimization evolves from the first quantization stage to the last, aiming for a deep understanding of optimization in learning-based PTQ schemes. Through extensive empirical studies spanning representative learning-based PTQ methods, LLM families, model scales, architectures, quantization settings and various tasks, we consistently uncover Optimization Imbalance: reconstruction loss magnitudes vary dramatically across stages, accompanied by highly uneven gradient magnitudes and parameter updates under MSE. We term the cross-stage range of loss magnitudes the reconstruction loss scale, and reveal that MSE translates the unexpectedly large reconstruction loss scale into highly uneven gradient magnitudes, which in turn lead to uneven optimization strength across quantization stages. This finding suggests a general principle for improving learning-based PTQ: optimization strength across stages should be decoupled from the reconstruction loss scale. Theoretically, we show that root mean squared error (RMSE) variants defined at the sample, channel, token, and element levels naturally realize this principle through implicit gradient normalization, outperforming MSE significantly as a drop-in replacement.

</details>


### [124] [Distilling Directional Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00997)
*Jungseob Lee,Sugyeong Eo,Seongtae Hong,Seungyoon Lee,Chanjun Park,Jaehyung Seo,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究引入了方向性标签蒸馏，通过冻结导师在已知方向上评分候选答案，而不是直接从生成的答案蒸馏，提高了学生模型在开放性准确性测试中的表现，尤其是在涉及父母和子女关系的事实时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的知识蒸馏方法在从生成答案中提取知识时可能会保留教师的局限性。为了克服这个缺点，作者提出了一种方向性标签蒸馏方法，通过让冻结的教师在已知方向上评分候选答案，并选择得分最高的作为学生的学习目标。

Method: 作者首先定义了方向性标签蒸馏的一系列步骤，包括冻结教师模型、基于已知方向进行评分、选择最佳候选作为学生模型的目标等。实验中使用了关于父母和子女的事实，通过对这些事实进行已知方向和请求方向的评分对比，展示了新方法的效果。

Result: 实验结果显示，使用已知方向评分的方法可以更准确地生成标签，尤其是在涉及父母和子女关系的特定事实时，即使对名字的先验进行了校正，其效果也超过了请求方向评分的方法，开放性准确性测试中的学生模型表现提高了13至15个点。

Conclusion: 研究表明，方向性验证通过提供更准确的训练目标，可以帮助减轻教师生成答案中的错误传递问题，从而提高了学生模型的表现。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation aims to transfer the factual knowledge of large language models to smaller models for efficient deployment. Yet a teacher may recall a relation in one direction while failing to generate the answer in the reverse direction. Distillation from its generated answers can therefore propagate this directional limitation to the student. The same teacher can nevertheless recognize such an answer by scoring the relation in the direction it knows. We introduce directional label distillation, in which frozen teachers score candidate answers in that known direction and the best-scoring candidate becomes the student's training target. On facts about parents and their children, known-direction scoring yields more accurate labels than scoring the requested direction, even after tuned corrections for name priors. With prior-corrected scores, the better direction depends on the facts rather than the template, and reverses on mined facts whose notable entity is the parent rather than the child. With the evaluated children's forward facts withheld, students trained on known-direction labels improve open-ended accuracy on their trained queries by 13 to 15 points over students trained on prior-corrected reverse labels. After generated answers are matched to a fixed name list by lexical similarity, students reproduce nearly all selected labels. Their accuracy largely follows label quality. The label advantage holds on unscreened queries and when candidates are retrieved without inserting correct answers. Our findings show that directional verification mitigates the transfer of errors from teacher-generated answers to students by providing more accurate training targets. Code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/directional-verification.

</details>


### [125] [Scaling and Distilling Text Embeddings for Better Diffusibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01016)
*Zekai Zhang,Yunjie Tian,Yanjin He,Xiaoyan Zhang,Dongdi Zhao,Qing Qu,Di Fu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了扩散语言模型（DLM）的最佳潜在空间，并通过缩小预训练模型的T5Gemma-2来改进生成性能。通过这种方法，构建了一个更连接且扩散性更好的潜在空间，从而提高了基于DLM的生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找扩散语言模型（DLM）中最佳的潜在空间，从而提升生成文本的质量。

Method: 通过对不同嵌入进行搜索，发现将相同的模型族（T5到T5Gemma-1再到T5Gemma-2）进行增强，可以显著提高生成性能；但原始的T5Gemma-2嵌入仍然不是最优的，因为它们过于区分，导致生成容易失败。为此，利用软标签对T5Gemma-2进行蒸馏，形成一个更紧密且扩散性更好的潜在空间。

Result: 通过蒸馏T5Gemma-2的嵌入，构建了一个中型DLM，其生成困惑度为17.8（相对于真实文本困惑度15.4），在RealWebText上的表现超过了GPT-2-M。

Conclusion: 作者的蒸馏方法明显提升了DLM的生成效果，为扩散模型在语言生成中的应用提供了新的见解。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) offer a promising alternative to autoregressive (AR) language generation. Recent advances in continuous DLMs, which apply latent diffusion to continuous text embeddings, raise a practical question: which embedding makes the best latent space, i.e., the most diffusible? To answer this, we search through different embeddings and find that scaling the embedding model to stronger ones within the same family (T5 to T5Gemma-1 to T5Gemma-2) greatly improves generative performance. But the raw T5Gemma-2 embeddings are still not optimal. They are so discriminative that even the embeddings of plausible alternative words are separated, which makes the generation vulnerable to imperfect sampling. Consequently, continuous diffusion often fails to reach any of them and ends up at an invalid embedding instead. To address this, we distill T5Gemma-2 into a student encoder that learns the teacher's decoded probabilities as soft labels. Learning from such soft labels makes the student pull the alternative embeddings closer while maintaining the encoding-decoding mechanism. The distilled embeddings form a more connected and diffusible latent space, improving over the vanilla T5Gemma-2 embeddings. As a result, our medium-sized DLM achieves Gen. PPL 17.8 (against real-text PPL 15.4) at real-text entropy on OpenWebText, outperforming GPT-2-M on Gen. PPL.

</details>


### [126] [It Takes Workflows to Evolve Better Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01026)
*Xuehang Guo,Haoyu Wang,Haifeng Chen,Yangyi Chen,Zhenhailong Wang,Qingyun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FloWright 提出了通过工作流作为框架来优化工作流的方法，采用分层、结构感知的奖励机制来实现单一角色的自我进化或多角色的共同进化，无需额外的模型、标签或执行。此外，提出 DataWright 方法将任务难度提升至多代理水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前方法只优化工作流生成器，而忽视了实际执行工作流的代理性能。FloWright 能够解决由于工作流的耦合性和单一分数结果导致的优化问题。

Method: FloWright 引入了分层、结构感知的奖励机制，允许一个角色自进化，多个角色共同进化。DataWright 则将单个代理能处理的数据集转换为多代理级别任务。

Result: 在文档、幻灯片、图表、代码、数学和金融任务中，使用 FloWright 训练的小型开放模型表现提升，最多提升 7.41%，且多个角色共同进化的模型相较于单独优化一个角色，性能提升更显著，最多可提升 5.03%。

Conclusion: FloWright 和 DataWright 为多代理工作流优化提供了新的途径，显著提升了模型在实际场景中的表现。

Abstract: Tackling complex real-world tasks can exceed the capabilities of a single large language model (LLM), motivating the use of multi-agent workflows that coordinate specialized agents to work together on these tasks. Recent methods train LLMs to construct better workflows from execution outcomes, but they optimize only the workflow generator, while the other agents that build or execute each workflow remain fixed even though every outcome depends on all of them. However, extending training beyond the generator is challenging: the agents are coupled, and a workflow's outcome is a single sparse score that cannot tell which agent causes a failure. We propose FloWright, which leverages the workflow as a harness to optimize workflows. By introducing a hierarchical, structure-aware reward paradigm, FloWright enables one role to self-evolve and two or more roles to co-evolve, with no additional models, labels, or executions. Considering the limitation that workflows are commonly trained and evaluated on data that a single agent can already handle, we further propose DataWright, an adaptive data hardening approach that converts existing datasets into workflow-level tasks with increased difficulty. Across document, slide, chart, code, math, and finance tasks, small open models trained with FloWright achieve improved performance by up to $+7.41\%$, with co-evolving ($+5.03\%$) more roles gaining more than optimizing one of them alone ($+2.83\%$). Our project page: https://xhguo7.github.io/FloWright/.

</details>


### [127] [LawCompass: Navigating from Legal QA to Multi-Agent Deep Research with Grounded Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01027)
*Xiaoxia Cheng,Linnan Wang,Jiahao Ma,Zhichuan Ye,Xuemei Zhou,Chuanyu Tong,Bo Jiang,Qing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LawCompass 是一种证据导向的法律助手，通过 Legal QA、Professional Retrieval 和 Deep Research 的多步骤推理和报告级综合，支持复杂的法律任务，同时保持所有模块间的明确引用链接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大多数法律助手主要局限于多轮对话型问答，无法处理需要系统证据检索、多步推理和报告级综合的复杂法律任务。为了克服这些限制，LawCompass 提供了一个新的框架，旨在通过多代理工作流程将标准法律问答向深层次研究过渡。

Method: LawCompass 采用多种方法实现其目标，包括 precise evidence-backed answers 提供准确的证据支持答案，通过查询重写进行结构化的法规和司法案例探索，以及利用多代理工作流程将复杂的法律任务分解并综合生成全面的研究报告。

Result: LawCompass 的评估结果表明，它提供了一种实用且可扩展的范式，可将对话式 AI 转变为具有证据导向属性和可信度的法律研究辅助。

Conclusion: LawCompass 的提出为复杂法律任务的处理提供了一个新颖且有效的解决方案，并展示了如何通过多代理系统和技术手段将传统法律问答平台升级为全面支持研究的平台。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) have significantly democratized access to legal information. Nevertheless, most existing legal assistants remain confined to multi-turn conversational QA, failing to support complex legal tasks that require systematic evidence retrieval, multi-step reasoning, and report-level synthesis. In this paper, we present LawCompass, an evidence-grounded legal assistant that navigates the transition from standard Legal QA to multi-agent deep research. LawCompass provides three task-oriented functions: Legal QA, which delivers precise, evidence-backed answers to legal questions; Professional Retrieval, which enables structured exploration of statutes and judicial cases via query rewriting; and Deep Research, which employs a multi-agent workflow to decompose complex legal tasks and synthesize comprehensive research reports. Crucially, LawCompass maintains explicit citation links across all modules, empowering users to directly verify system outputs against original legal sources. Evaluation results demonstrate that LawCompass provides a practical and scalable paradigm for transforming conversational AI into trustworthy and evidence-grounded legal research assistance.

</details>


### [128] [Sentence Specificity Scores for Collaborative Technical Documentation: A Domain-Transfer Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01046)
*Rocker D'Antonio,Thomas Benton Townsend,Dimitrios Michael Manias*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析了具体性评分在技术文档中的表现，并测试了固定领域预测器和目标适应预测器的效果。这些模型在不同语料库中的排序结果不同，但同句排名的一致性较高。使用这些评分可以提高选择方向正确的修订版本的比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于分析具体性评分在技术文档中的作用，以及这些评分是否能帮助选择最合适的AI生成的修订版本。具体性评分的精准度对于准确传达信息至关重要。

Method: 研究方法包括审计句子具体性评分的偏差，并通过固定领域预测器SpeciTeller和目标适应预测器，对Wikipedia及三个技术文档语料库进行评分。同时，通过调整严格筛选和词干长度来改变评分模式。

Result: 结果表明，固定的一般领域预测器SpeciTeller和目标适应预测器在不同语料库中的排序不同，但同句排名一致性较高。调整评分模式未能统一这些差异。但在Gemma集合中，SpeciTeller评分提高了方向正确修订版本的选择率，在GPT-OSS-120B集合中则有所降低。

Conclusion: 研究结论指出，具体性评分的解释和决策价值取决于预测器和候选集的选择。这些评分需要针对特定情况优化才能更好地发挥作用。

Abstract: Collaboration depends on shared context, and technical documentation is one way that context persists across people and AI teammates. Specificity, the amount and exactness of detail expressed in language, shapes what information documentation captures and how precisely that information is communicated. This work audits sentence-specificity scoring artifacts on technical documentation and tests whether scores applied only after generation help choose among fixed LLM-generated revisions. Across Wikipedia and three technical-documentation corpora, the fixed general-domain predictor SpeciTeller and the pinned post-publication author-repository implementation of Ko et al.'s target-adapted predictor produce different corpus orders and same-sentence rank agreement from -0.066 to 0.510. Strict filtering and token-length adjustment change these patterns without reconciling them. In the Gemma set, SpeciTeller ranking raises direction-valid selection from 71.7% to 83.3% (+11.7 points; 95% source-case bootstrap interval +1.7 to +21.7); in the GPT-OSS-120B set, SpeciTeller ranking raises direction-valid selection from 51.7% to 56.7% (+5.0 points; 95% source-case bootstrap interval -6.7 to +16.7), and every primary single-score GPT-OSS-120B interval includes zero. These findings tie score interpretation and decision value to the predictor and candidate set.

</details>


### [129] [Capturing In-Context Learning Dynamics with Task Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01054)
*Guangzhi Xiong,Zhenghao He,Bohan Liu,Sanchit Sinha,Wenqian Ye,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过引入Task Operator（TO），展示了如何更高效地执行In-context learning（ICL），它在零样本推理和ICL之间的性能差距上表现尤为突出，并且能够在不增加上下文窗口大小的情况下实现有效的多样本扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ICL方法在执行新任务时依赖于全集示例的处理，导致部署效率低下。TO方法旨在通过将注意力头的输出视为上下文掩码输出的仿射变换，找出这种变换的参数在给定任务中是稳定的，进而提出TO来实现更有效的ICL执行。

Method: TO方法通过仿射变换来实现ICL的高效执行，具体包括分析ICL前向传播过程，识别每个注意力头输出是其上下文掩码输出的仿射变换，并基于此提出TO，通过解析推导出的更新方式来应用于注意力输出投影。

Result: 通过TO方法，研究在多种任务（包括词汇、算法和推理任务）中实现了最佳性能，并显著缩小了零样本推理和ICL之间的差距。此外，还发现提取的知识在任务特定的稀疏电路中集中在不同层和位置，通过来自不连续演示批次的运算符平均，可以在不扩大上下文窗口的情况下实现有效的多样本扩展。

Conclusion: TO方法揭示了ICL机制的更多细节，展示了如何更高效地执行ICL，并提供了有效扩展ICL的新途径。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) enables language models to perform new tasks from demonstrations without weight updates. However, every ICL inference requires processing the full set of examples, resulting in inefficient deployments, and how ICL works mechanistically is not fully understood. Prior work compresses ICL into fixed activation vectors extracted from specific layers or positions, but these input-independent interventions fail on complex tasks where the output depends on fine-grained interactions with the input. By analyzing the ICL forward pass, we show that each attention head's output is an affine transformation of its context-masked counterpart, and that the parameters of this transformation are empirically stable across samples for a given task. Building on this, we introduce Task Operator (TO), which replays this transformation as an analytically derived update to the attention output projection. Across lexical, algorithmic, and reasoning tasks, TO achieves the best overall performance among prior methods and substantially narrows the gap between zero-shot inference and ICL. We further show that the extracted knowledge concentrates in a task-specific sparse circuit across layers and positions, and that averaging operators from disjoint demonstration batches enables effective many-shot scaling without expanding the context window. Our code is available at https://github.com/gzxiong/task_operator.

</details>


### [130] [JoinGR: Learning to Traverse Join Graphs for Table Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01064)
*Sandipan De,Abhijit Chakraborty,Sambaran Bandyopadhyay,Vivek Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: JOINGR 是一种针对表格检索的方法，利用数据库的连接图进行表的选择与评分，从而提高Text-to-SQL任务中表格检索的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的密集型表格检索方法单独评估模式元素，忽略了通过加入关系到已相关表的未提及关键表所提供的证据。

Method: JOINGR 使用数据库连接图作为检索空间，将列表示为图节点，并用带类型的关系边表示表内部的关系以及外部键关系。它通过一个根据查询条件评分的轻量级 MLP 来选择语义上相似的基础表，遍历连接边，并将结果的边存款合成为表评分。

Result: 在 BIRD 和 Spider 数据集上，JOINGR 与最强的检索基线具有竞争力。在具有多跳表需求的挑战性企业基准 BEAVER 上，JOINGR 显著提高了召回率。跨域实验表明，学到的评分器在不同基准上具有可迁移性。

Conclusion: JOINGR 方法在表格检索任务中表现良好，特别是在具有多跳表需求的场景下，为进一步提升 Text-to-SQL 任务的效果奠定了基础。

Abstract: Retrieving the right tables is a prerequisite for Text-to-SQL over realistic databases. Dense table retrievers rank schema elements independently, but this ignores a key source of evidence: some required tables are not mentioned in the question and become identifiable only through their join relationships to already relevant tables. We introduce JOINGR, a join-aware table retrieval method that treats the database join graph as the retrieval space. Columns are represented as graph nodes, while intra-table and foreign-key relationships are represented as typed edges. Given a question, JOINGR selects semantically similar anchor tables, traverses join edges with a query-conditioned scorer, and aggregates the resulting edge deposits into table scores. The scorer is a lightweight MLP on top of frozen query, node, and edge embeddings, trained with a pairwise margin loss over gold tables. On BIRD and Spider datasets, JOINGR is competitive with the strongest retrieval baselines. On BEAVER, a challenging enterprise benchmark with multi-hop table requirements, JOINGR substantially improves recall over dense retrieval and re-ranking baselines. Cross-domain experiments show that the learned scorer transfers across benchmarks, indicating that the method captures reusable joingraph traversal behavior.

</details>


### [131] [Probe with Participation Trophies: Random-Reward RL as a Probe of LLM Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01066)
*Yu Mao,Lei Yu,Zining Zhu,Yusheng Zheng,Haohang Li,Freda Shi,Yutong Yin,Zhaoran Wang,Jingcheng Niu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究将无奖励奖励的强化学习与模型的可达性联系起来，提出了无奖励奖励的强化学习作为工具来探索模型的能力，揭示了不同类型训练阶段的不同表现模式，并解决了解码探针中标签泄露的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决模型性能与数据污染或特定机制之间的长期争议，提出了通过无奖励奖励的强化学习来探究模型的可达性。

Method: 分析了大型语言模型在强化学习任务中的表现，并通过OMLo检查点测试其在不同训练阶段的表现。

Result: 研究发现，早中期训练中，随机奖励可以显著提升模型表现；不同训练阶段下，模型的行为模式呈现不同特征。

Conclusion: 无奖励奖励的强化学习为探索模型的进一步能力提供了一种新视角，有助于理解模型在缺乏正确反馈时的实际能力。

Abstract: We connect the spurious-reward paradox to a model's reachability and propose random-reward reinforcement learning (RL) as a useful tool for the probing enterprise, addressing a decade-long debate over what probing performance actually reveals about a model. There are two prevailing explanations for the surprising finding that even random rewards can improve the performance of large language models (LLMs): one attributes the gains to particular mechanisms within RL training; the other to data contamination. Our results motivate a different view: spurious-reward RL can probe a model's reachability, or what further training can attain from its current state under specified constraints, beyond what is reflected in its current performance. Two OLMo checkpoints with the same accuracy on synthetic arithmetic (3.5%), for example, reach 8.5% and 55% in their best runs under the same correctness-rewarded RL. Examining OLMo checkpoints across pre-training and mid-training reveals three distinct regimes of training response: early on, RL produces little improvement even when correct answers are rewarded; later in pre-training, rewarding correct answers becomes effective while random rewards remain weak; and, upon entering mid-training, even random rewards can produce large gains. A similar ordering appears in a number-masked supervised fine-tuning (SFT) analysis of these checkpoints, suggesting that the pattern is not specific to a particular RL mechanism. Moreover, RL with random rewards offers a distinctive perspective on what training can make an LLM do, since its reward signal supplies no information about which answers are correct. By asking what training can attain without correctness feedback, it addresses the label-leakage side of a central problem in decodability-based probing: whether a successful probe reveals the model's capabilities or learns the task itself.

</details>


### [132] [Precision over Scale: A Polish-Silesian Benchmark and a Translation System Outperforming Open-Source and Commercial Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01082)
*Grzegorz Kulik,Mikołaj Pokrywka,Adam Jatowt,Wojciech Kusa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对波兰语-西里西亚方言的机器翻译挑战，使用神经和规则系统进行评估，并发布了新的SiLTT测试集和最佳神经模型，以支持进一步研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于方言数据稀缺且语法规则性强，现有的机器翻译基准假设文本是标准化和精校的，这给方言机器翻译带来了挑战。因此，研究者旨在评估标准和基于规则的方法在特定方言上的表现。

Method: 该研究使用了神经机器翻译系统和基于规则的方法，评估了它们在波兰语-西里西亚方言翻译任务上的性能，并利用了新构建的SiLTT测试集以及BOUQuET和FLORES标准基准。

Result: 结果表明基于规则的方法在SiLTT和BOUQuET的数据集上表现最好。而针对特定数据集进行微调的TranslateGemma在神经基线之上有所改进，但这并没有在方言环境中超越基于规则的方法。

Conclusion: 研究结论认为，尽管微调的神经模型在一些标准测试集上表现良好，但在方言翻译任务上，基于规则的方法仍可能更为有效。此外，研究还发布了一个新的测试集SiLTT和他们的最佳神经模型，以促进进一步研究。

Abstract: Dialectal machine translation remains challenging due to limited data and strong linguistic variation not captured by standard benchmarks, which often assume standardized and well-edited text. We study Polish-Silesian MT using neural and rule-based systems, evaluating on SiLTT - a new Pol-Szl testset, alongside established BOUQuET and FLORES benchmarks. Results show our rule-based system is consistently strongest on SiLTT and BOUQuET datasets and that TranslateGemma fine-tuned on a curated dataset improves over strong neural baselines but does not surpass the rule-based system in dialectal settings. We release SiLTT and our best neural model to support further research.

</details>


### [133] [AgSpec: Pushing the Limits of Retrieval-Based Speculative Decoding in Coding Agent Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01108)
*Sumin Lee,Sukmin Cho,Suengjae Lim,Youngjin Kwon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AgSpec 提供了一个框架，解决了现有检索驱动推测解码方法在代码生成管道中的不足之处，如缺乏合适的语料库和可变的草案长度策略，从而提高了代码生成的速度和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的检索驱动推测解码方法在编码代理管道中存在不足，因为大量的可重用文本数据在它们的语料库中缺失或者存储形式与代理的输出形式不同，且现有的方法忽略了不同代理间草案长度存在差异并且具有漂移变化的情况。因此，需要一个能够解决这些不足的系统框架 AgSpec。

Method: AgSpec 通过从会话、工作区和全局语料库中检索，并保持会话轨迹，对标记打开文件使用代理的输出格式进行索引存储，同时为每个代理设定一个基于离线配置的草案长度上限，并通过验证反馈在线调整长度。

Result: 在两个基于仓库的大规模多代理编码基准测试中，AgSpec 在大部分评估设置下比五种检索基础的推测解码器和 EAGLE-3 都表现更好，提高了自动回归解码的生成吞吐量，最高可提升 4.76 倍（批处理大小为 16 的情况下）。此外，AgSpec 也在没有仓库或多代理管道的基准测试中有效，展示了其在不同编码代理中的普适性。

Conclusion: AgSpec 通过增强基于检索的编译解码系统的语料库供给和草案长度策略，显著提高了多代理编程环境中的代码生成效率，并且这种改进对于不同的编码代理都是有效的。

Abstract: Retrieval-based speculative decoding (SD) drafts tokens by copying continuations from existing text, which suits coding agents that repeatedly reproduce code, logs, and earlier attempts. Yet existing methods fall short in agent pipelines: much of the reusable text is missing from their corpora or stored in a form that differs from what the agent emits, and their draft lengths ignore that accept length varies across agents and drifts over turns. We present AgSpec, a framework that supplies the corpus and draft-length policies that existing retrieval engines lack in coding-agent pipelines. AgSpec retrieves from session, workspace, and global corpora, retaining the ongoing session trajectory and indexing opened files in the agent's emission format. It bounds each agent's draft length with an offline-profiled cap and adapts the length online from verification feedback. On two repository-level multi-agent coding benchmarks, AgSpec outperforms five retrieval-based drafters and EAGLE-3 in most evaluated settings, raising generation throughput over autoregressive decoding up to 4.37$\times$ at batch size 1 and 4.76$\times$ at batch size 16. AgSpec also remains effective on benchmarks without a repository or a multi-agent pipeline, showing that its gains generalize to coding agents broadly.

</details>


### [134] [Madeleine: Learning Involuntary Recall for Conversational Memory from Simulated Lives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01118)
*Zhiyun Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Madeleine 是一个无需 LLM 调用的学习到的关联系统，通过离线模拟人类生活场景来学习记忆关联，并在线实时处理查询，显著提高了多种系统的性能，同时降低了对上下文信息的需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的系统依赖于 LLM 的实时或读取时推理来建立关联，这会带来大量的 LLM 调用和上下文信息需求。因此，提出了一个新的学习到的关联系统 Madeleine，以减少对 LLM 的依赖，并提高系统的效率。

Method: Madeleine 离线通过 LLM 模拟人类生活生成模拟情景，通过这些情景中的线索-触发器对来训练一个查询编码器来获取残差关联，从而替代固定的相似性。在线阶段，Madeleine 直接使用离线学习到的关联，无需再次调用 LLM，并可插入任何向量记忆系统。

Result: 在 LoCoMo-Plus 评测下，Madeleine 系统在 HyperMem 上达到 66.6 的得分，而单独使用的得分与 HyperMem 的官方发行分数（52.4 vs. 52.9）相当，但不需要任何 LLM 调用，且仅需 HyperMem 答案上下文的 1/21。相较于未训练的 T-Mem，Madeleine 提升了 26.2 分，显著优于未训练的同一体系，并在 4B 表面上保持了一般的 QA 能力。

Conclusion: Madeleine 通过学习到的关联策略有效地降低了对 LLM 的依赖，提高了系统的性能，同时减少了对上下文信息的要求。这表明通过离线学习和在线应用的方法可以取得显著成效。

Abstract: A long-term conversational assistant must recall the right memory at the right moment, yet the memory that matters most is often not similar to what the user says now. Current systems recover such associations by letting an LLM reason at write or read time, at a cost of hundreds to over a thousand LLM calls per memory bank and up to several thousand context tokens per query. We argue that association is a learnable relevance: the pointwise mutual information of memories under how human lives unfold. We introduce Madeleine, which learns amortized association: offline, an LLM life simulator writes simulated lives, whose cue-trigger pairs teach a query encoder a residual association on top of frozen similarity; online, it calls no LLM and plugs into any vector memory by replacing only the query encoder. On LoCoMo-Plus under the official protocol, Madeleine (I) reaches 66.6 when plugged into HyperMem, the highest among all systems evaluated under this protocol; (II) used alone, reaches the score of HyperMem as released (52.4 vs. 52.9) with zero LLM calls and about 1/21 of its answer context; and (III) lifts T-Mem by 26.2 points, significantly outperforms the same untrained backbone inside both systems, and leaves ordinary QA intact on the 4B backbone.

</details>


### [135] [Counting and Min-Cost Encoding for Tokenization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01127)
*Shuming Shi,Xiang Zhang,Hao Yu,Wenbo Fei,Changjian Wang,Zhan Wang,Guoqing Pang,Guangye Yu,Quan Lu,Ning Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新的分词器训练方法CNF和文编码算法MCE，能够提高分词效率和可扩展性，并且在不牺牲下游任务性能的情况下提高了压缩率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流的大型语言模型依赖于分词器将文本编码为词汇表序列，不同的分词器导致同样的文本可能被编码成不同长度的序列，而序列长度直接影响推理时间。因此，研究人员提出了新的方法CNF-MCE来优化分词效率。

Method: CNF-MCE的分词方法包括：(1)利用直接计数有效子字符串生成初始词汇表；(2)通过过滤器基于实际分词训练语料的最佳分割来构造最终的词汇表。并且定义了成本函数以在分割文本时全局最小化整体成本。

Result: 在六个文本分类和两个词汇表大小组中，CNF-MCE持续优于评估的BPE分词器，在250K词汇表大小时相较于o200k_base和qwen250k分词器，提高了26%和30%的压缩率。扩展至1M词汇表时，显示出对BPE的持续性能改善，提高超过60%的分词效率，并且词汇表利用率从52.9%提升到96.9%。从空白训练的1.8B和8B规模的语言模型能够在11个基准测试中表现出与使用BPE分词器的模型相当的平均性能。

Conclusion: 总的来说，CNF-MCE可以在保持良好的下游任务性能的同时提高词汇表的利用率和压缩率，具有更高的效率和更好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Mainstream large language models rely on a tokenizer to encode text into a token sequence. Different tokenizers may yield token sequences of substantially different lengths for the same text. With a fixed model architecture, shorter token sequences correspond to lower inference time. We propose a tokenizer training approach named Counting and Filtering (CNF) and a text encoding algorithm called Min-Cost Encoding (MCE). MCE defines a cost function over a text segment, and determines the best segmentation by globally minimizing the overall segmentation cost. CNF builds a raw vocabulary by directly counting valid substrings, and then constructs the final vocabulary through a filtering step based on actual token usage when segmenting the training corpus with MCE. The CNF-MCE conbination offers several advantages over BPE, including higher token efficiency, greater scalability, and lower dependency. Across six text categories and two vocabulary-size groups, CNF-MCE consistently achieves better compression than the evaluated BPE tokenizers. With a 250K vocabulary, CNF-MCE increases compression rate by 26% and 30% on English web text over the o200k_base and qwen250k tokenizers. Experiments scaling the vocabulary to 1M entries on English web text demonstrate sustained improvements over BPE, with a token efficiency improvement of over 60% and vocabulary utilization rising from 52.9% to 96.9%. The MCE algorithm does not depend on a merge list (as in BPE) or token probability (as in UnigramLM), making it applicable to a wide range of vocabularies, including those built from BPE, UnigramLM, CNF, and others. Language models trained from scratch at the 1.8B and 8B scales achieve comparable average performance to models using the BPE tokenizers across 11 benchmarks. These results demonstrate that CNF-MCE can improve token efficiency significantly while maintaining competitive downstream performance.

</details>


### [136] [BanglaDial-Abuse: A Corpus-Grounded Dataset for Regional Dialect Identification in Abusive Bangla Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01150)
*Hasin Almas Sifat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 介绍了BanglaDial-Abuse数据集，一个包含标准孟加拉语、喀什米尔语、齐利特和巴瑞萨尔四种方言的孟加拉语书写语料库。该数据集用于识别非正式和非标准文本中的攻击性和敌对性孟加拉语文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在解决孟加拉语自然语言处理中区域语言变体的挑战，特别是在非正式和非标准文本中。通过创建一个包含区域方言差异的数据集，可以更好地理解并处理这些方言。

Method: 通过使用基于语料库的合成过程，结合方言间的代词、所有格形式、动词形态、否定、疑问句结构、方位词、词汇和孟加拉语书写规则的变化，但保留其攻击性和敌对性语义。

Result: 生成了一个包含1000个句子的数据集，每个方言类包含250个样本，显示四种方言在词汇层面存在差异。各两两相关词汇相似度介于0.37到0.56之间。

Conclusion: 当前版本的数据集被设计为研究和原型制作的语料库而非经过原住民验证的权威语言资源，主要用于多类方言识别任务，而非二元攻击性文本检测任务。

Abstract: Regional linguistic variation remains an important challenge for Bangla natural language processing, particularly in informal and non-standard text. This paper introduces BanglaDial-Abuse, a balanced Bengali-script dataset developed for regional dialect identification in abusive and hostile Bangla text. The dataset contains 1,000 sentences distributed equally across four linguistic varieties: Standard Bangla, Chattagram, Sylhet, and Barishal, with 250 samples per class. The resource was constructed using a corpus-grounded synthetic procedure incorporating regional variation in pronouns, possessive forms, verb morphology, negation, interrogative structures, postpositions, vocabulary, and Bengali-script spelling conventions while preserving the underlying hostile or abusive meaning. Descriptive analysis shows broadly comparable sentence-length distributions but partially distinct lexical spaces across the four classes. Pairwise Jaccard vocabulary similarity ranges from 0.37 to 0.56. The primary task is four-class regional dialect identification rather than binary abusive-text detection. The dataset is publicly available through Zenodo under a Creative Commons Attribution 4.0 license. The current version is intended as a research and prototyping corpus rather than a native-speaker-validated gold-standard linguistic resource. Keywords: Bangla, Bengali, dialect identification, regional dialect, abusive language, low-resource NLP, Chattagram, Sylhet, Barishal, dataset

</details>


### [137] [My FAULT: Self-Diagnosis as Credit Assignment in Self-Evolving Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01161)
*Yihua Zhu,Qianying Liu,Weixu Qiao,Xuan Ren,Weiwei Xu,Wenbo Li,Wei Wang,Ruijia Chen,Xinmiao Luan,Yin Luo,Hao Huang,Xiang Zheng,Hidetoshi Shimodaira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为FAULT的方法，通过利用自我诊断信息和最近任务结果在线更新错误成本，解决了终端奖励信号分配中的问题，特别是在长时任务中重现学习信号并细化错误责任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法在使用终端奖励进行训练时出现了信用分配问题，尤其是在长时间任务中，这一问题表现得尤为明显。

Method: 本文提出了一种方法，称为Self-Diagnosis-guided Terminal Credit Redistribution（简称FAULT），它将诊断出的错误转换为明确的步骤级信用，并将其与终端结果挂钩，同时从任务结果中学习相对错误成本，使策略和自我诊断者共同进化。

Result: 在ALFWorld数据集上，FAULT能够恢复相同的终端奖励组的学习信号，其覆盖率为95%，而GRPO和GiGPO分别为41%和72%。此外，FAULT在特定错误步骤上实现了更精细的错误定位，并在长时间任务ALFWorld和WebShop上表现出了明显的优势，同时在短时任务上保持了竞争力。

Conclusion: 总的来说，FAULT通过利用自我诊断信息和对最近任务结果的学习，显著改进了信用分配问题，尤其是对于长时间任务，在多个评估任务上取得了良好效果。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful approach for training large language model agents on multi-step tasks, yet reliance on terminal outcome rewards creates two credit-assignment problems, particularly in long-horizon tasks. First, same-outcome rollout groups provide no learning signal from terminal rewards. Second, terminal rewards provide only trajectory-wide feedback, making it difficult to identify which decisions caused a failure. Recent work supplements terminal rewards with finer-grained information from trajectory analysis, such as natural-language reflections on intermediate decisions and errors. However, natural-language diagnoses are difficult to use directly for credit assignment: their error claims may be unreliable, and they do not quantify how much each error should affect learning. We propose Self-Diagnosis-guided Terminal Credit Redistribution (FAULT), which turns diagnosed errors into explicit step-level credit anchored by terminal outcomes. FAULT checks diagnostic evidence and learns relative error costs from task outcomes. During training, the policy and self-diagnoser co-evolve, while error costs are updated online from recent outcomes. On ALFWorld, FAULT recovers learning signals from same-outcome groups, reaching 95% signal coverage versus 41% for GRPO and 72% for GiGPO, while better localizing credit to specific error steps. Across two model scales, FAULT delivers strong. improvements on the long-horizon ALFWorld and WebShop tasks while remaining competitive on short-horizon Search-based QA.

</details>


### [138] [HeadEdit: Calibrating Language Model Behavior Through the Frozen Unembedding Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01170)
*Zirui He,Haiyan Zhao,Jingyu Hu,Yinghao Wu,Chenxi Yuan,Yingcong Li,Yandong Bai,Mengnan Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HeadEdit 是一种无需梯度的方法，用于通过解码矩阵调整语言模型的行为，能够在不显著增加推理开销的情况下改善模型在九个实验设置中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的对齐方法主要将行为错误视为计算问题，HeadEdit 则旨在探索模型内部是否已经存在预期的行为

Method: HeadEdit 通过从配对完成中提取低秩行为子空间，并利用每个提示的坐标生成词汇级别的修正，从而实现隐式的自适应引导。

Result: HeadEdit 在三个任务的三个模型家族的所有九个实验设置中表现优异，几乎没有增加推理开销，并且在调优后仍能保持性能。

Conclusion: HeadEdit 提供了一种实际、轻量且可解释的方法，用于通过解码矩阵调整语言模型的行为。

Abstract: Alignment does not eliminate behavioral errors in language models. Models may still refuse benign requests, call unnecessary tools, or yield to false user claims. Current methods mitigate such errors as a computation problem, and rarely explore if the desired behavior is already encoded in the model's representation. Motivated by the observation that behavior-relevant information remains linearly decodable from the final hidden state even when the resulting logits produce the undesired behavior, we introduce HeadEdit, a gradient-free method that calibrates model behavior through the unembedding matrix. HeadEdit extracts a low-rank behavioral subspace from paired completions and uses each prompt's coordinates within it to generate a vocabulary-wide correction, thereby implementing implicitly adaptive steering without manually specified target tokens or parameter updates. HeadEdit improves all nine experimental settings across three tasks and three model families, with negligible inference overhead and no systematic loss of general capabilities. It also reveals a connection to gradient-based alignment. HeadEdit's low-dimensional representation partly predicts how preference tuning changes output logits on unseen prompts. The subspace learned from the model can also be reused after tuning, improving performance without re-extracting or retuning. These results show that HeadEdit provides a practical, lightweight, and interpretable way to calibrate model behavior through the unembedding matrix.

</details>


### [139] [Temporally-Resolved Token Attribution Reveals the Generation Dynamics of Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01177)
*Darpan Aswal,Céline Hudelot*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DLIG 是一种适用于生成式语言模型的解释方法，扩展了集成梯度方法到任意层和去噪步骤，帮助理解模型决策过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏适用于生成式语言模型的解释方法，DLIG 方法旨在填补这一空白。

Method: DLIG通过将集成梯度方法应用到生成式语言模型的每个去噪步骤和层次中，为模型的自我生成或固定完成部分输入提示提供逐令牌的归因。

Result: DLIG在词义消歧、多跳图推理和句子填充任务中的表现展示了模型是如何跨位置、层次和去噪步骤使用输入信息的。

Conclusion: DLIG作为一种轻量级方法，能够作为介入分析的补充，提供一个低成本的机制假设检验工具，揭示生成式语言模型的工作原理。

Abstract: This work presents Diffusion Layer Integrated Gradients (DLIG), a token attribution method for diffusion language models (DLMs) that extends Integrated Gradients (IG~\cite{sundararajan2017axiomatic}) to arbitrary layers and denoising steps. DLIG attributes a DLM's progressive commitment to a self-generated or fixed completion for an input prompt. We establish direct correspondences between DLIG and the IG axioms of completeness, implementation invariance, linearity, and symmetry preservation. As a lightweight complement to interventional analysis, DLIG provides an inexpensive first check of mechanistic hypotheses across the denoising trajectory. We demonstrate this on word-sense disambiguation, multi-hop graph reasoning, and sentence infilling, revealing how DLMs draw on inputs across positions, layers, and denoising steps.

</details>


### [140] [AGO AI Quality Gate: Evidence-First Release Decisions for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01218)
*Giulio Zeloni,Enrico Lo Conte,Salvatore Rionero,Giuseppe Santoro,Alessandro Rastelli,Fabio Sorrentino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AGO框架提供了一种基于证据的质量门控方法，通过整合四种核心组件来提升生成系统的质量评估。该方法在公开基准RAGBench上进行评估，表明不同级别的语言模型在不同领域的性能差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对企业使用检索增强生成（RAG）系统时必须做出的重复运营决策，即推动、修订或阻止系统版本，该决策基于不完整的证据和多变的LLM评估，缺乏系统性和可靠性。

Method: AGO引入了一种证据优先的质量门控框架，包含四种关键组件：四状态决策模型，综合积分评分系统，基于贝塔-二项分布的分层门控机制，以及强制性的元评估协议，以验证LLM评估的质量。框架通过与RAGBench数据集的对比测试，展示了其在不同语言模型中的应用效果。

Result: 研究表明，低成本模型gpt-4.1-nano在检测不规范答案时的效能远低于较高成本的模型gpt-4o，这表明选择合适的评估模型对于提高评估正确性至关重要。同时，在不同领域，gpt-4o的表现差异也揭示了质量评估的复杂性。

Conclusion: 基于这些结果，研究者强调在系统发布决策时，不应仅依赖点估计值，而应该考虑逐次活动的质量评估，并设计适用于多个领域的高质量评估框架。

Abstract: Enterprises adopting retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) face a recurring operational decision: promote, revise, or block a system version. The evidence is incomplete and the metrics come from fallible LLM judges. We report on AGO AI Quality Gate (AGO), an evidence-first quality-gate framework deployed in industrial RAG assessment engagements. AGO integrates four key components: a four-state decision model that treats missing data and judge errors as explicit outcomes; layered scoring combining deterministic checks, local guardrails, and structured LLM evaluation; a stratified beta-binomial gate that quantifies regression risk probabilistically; and a mandatory meta-evaluation protocol to validate the LLM judge before it influences decisions. Since engagement data is proprietary, we evaluate the judge layer on RAGBench, a public benchmark of 100k annotated RAG traces across 12 datasets. On identical stratified test samples (N=1200 per judge), a low-cost judge (gpt-4.1-nano) detects non-adherent answers barely above chance (AUROC 0.603 [0.570, 0.634]), despite producing flawless protocol output, while gpt-4o reaches 0.783 [0.756, 0.807] -- yet its per-domain performance still ranges from 0.62 to 0.88. A fixed-seed gate study spanning regression, no change, and improvement quantifies unsafe promotion, false-alarm cost, and improvement throughput. Under regression, the decision-grade profile reduces unsafe promotion to 22.2%-35.1%, against 29.3%-41.8% for a naive gate. These results support the design choices that judge quality must be measured per engagement and that point estimates alone are not a release decision.

</details>


### [141] [ASCRIBE: Atomic and Significance-Based Reasoning for Thai Clinical SOAP Note Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01234)
*Tarm Kalavantavanich,Teerawut Ponarchar,Pattaramanee Arsomngern,Jenta Wonglertsakul,Watcharakorn Chuthong,Chiraphat Boonnag,Knot Pipatsrisawat,Titipat Achakulvisut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ASCRIBE 提出了一种新型的临床推理框架，能够在提取信息时分配临床显著性级别，使其能够更好地为通用语言模型生成可靠的医疗文档。此外，还发布了首个脱敏的泰语临床总结基准数据集 ThaiClinicBench 及其合成训练语料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理方法常常忽略了临床重要的信息且生成了未支持的内容，阻碍了泰国在自动 SOAP 报告生成中的进步。

Method: ASCRIBE 设计了一个基于临床医生启发的推理框架，对对话中的每个提取的原子事实赋予一个临床显著性级别，以此来增强通用语言模型的工作表现。

Result: ASCRIBE 在 GPT-5.4 和 Gemini 3.1 Pro 上的表现优于链式思维提示，并在完整性度量上比标准提示高出 10.3 分。此外，一个仅使用合成数据进行训练的 Gemma-4-E4B 模型利用 ASCRIBE 的奖励机制，在事实精确度和完整性方面分别达到了与 Gemini 3.1 Pro 相当和超越的水平。

Conclusion: ASCRIBE 通过引入临床显著性级别，提高了通用语言模型生成医疗文档的质量，尤其在医疗完整性方面显著提高，并且通过开源数据集和代码支持了这一研究的推广和验证。

Abstract: Automatic SOAP note generation can ease the documentation burden on physicians, but existing reasoning methods often omit clinically important information and generate unsupported content. Progress in Thai is further hindered by the lack of publicly available datasets. We propose ASCRIBE, a physician-inspired reasoning framework that ascribes a clinical-significance level to each extracted atomic fact in the conversation before summarization, making a general-purpose LLM a more reliable scribe. We also release ThaiClinicBench, the first de-identified Thai clinical summarization benchmark of real encounters, together with a synthetic training corpus derived from real clinical notes. As a prompt, ASCRIBE outperforms chain-of-thought prompting on GPT-5.4 and Gemini 3.1 Pro across the physician-aligned LLM-judge metrics and improves on standard prompting by up to 10.3 points on the completeness LLM-judge metric. As a GRPO reward, it enables a Gemma-4-E4B model trained solely on synthetic data to match Gemini 3.1 Pro in factual precision and surpass it in completeness. Code and data can be found at https://github.com/loolootech/ascribe.

</details>


### [142] [Harness Annealing: Learning to Act with Less External Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01235)
*Yingxuan Yang,Huacan Chai,Ying Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究引入了HAT（Harness Annealing Training）方法，通过逐渐减弱外部操控的干预，训练模型学习承担控制职责并在需要时独立运行。研究表明，这种方法有助于减少模型任务执行时所需的实时干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型依赖外部工具进行状态跟踪和决策，培训中使用这些工具提升了任务性能，但决策仍需实时干预。研究提出一种新的训练方法HAT，旨在让模型学会自身承担这些控制职责，进而减少运行时的人为干预需求。

Method: 该方法包括目标控制监督和一个逐渐减弱外部操控的课程，实验通过收集不同强度的操控环境下的指导轨迹进行模型训练，评估最终模型在无操控环境下的表现。

Result: 使用不同规模的模型（9B和35B）和不同评估配置在SWE-QA和SWE-QA-Pro任务上的试验结果表明，部分经过HAT训练的模型在无操控环境下的表现接近或接近年初训练状态下有操控支持的表现。不同模型配置和规模下，HAT带来的好处有所不同，进一步减弱操控并不一定会持续提升性能。

Conclusion: HAT方法展示了利用外部支持的经验帮助模型学会减少自身操作，在一定条件下可以减少模型在实际应用中的实时干预需求，但仍需优化以适用于更广泛的模型规模和任务配置。

Abstract: Language agents rely on external harnesses to track state, organize workflows, and verify answers. Beyond providing tools and information, these harnesses supply control decisions about what to investigate, whether to revise, and when to stop. Training on successful harness-supported trajectories can improve task performance while leaving these decisions dependent on runtime intervention. We ask whether harness-supported experience can also teach the model to make these decisions, allowing the division of control to change as the model learns. We call this objective harness internalization: learning to assume specified control responsibilities while retaining task performance after the corresponding support is withdrawn. We introduce HARNESS ANNEALING TRAINING (HAT), which combines explicit control supervision with a curriculum over teacher trajectories collected under progressively weaker harnesses. Experiments with 9B and 35B models on SWE-QA and SWE-QA-Pro evaluate every checkpoint under four deployment harnesses. Selected annealed checkpoints operating with tools alone achieve scores close to those of their respective starting checkpoints deployed with the full harness. The benefits vary with model scale and deployment configuration, and further annealing does not uniformly improve performance. These findings suggest that harness-supported experience can help reduce the runtime control required by a trained agent.

</details>


### [143] [Evaluating the Robustness of Japanese LLMs to IME-Related and Typographical Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01241)
*Ryota Mibayashi,Hiroaki Ohshima*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究评估了日本大型语言模型在面对特定日语打字错误时的鲁棒性，发现字符转换和替换错误显著降低准确性，而IME转换、全角转换和同音词转换的影响较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的动机在于探索大型语言模型在多种自然语言处理任务中的表现，尤其是当涉及到日语这种包含多个书写系统的语言时，模型对打字错误的鲁棒性不足。

Method: 研究通过在三个日语基准数据集（JMMLU、JCommonsenseQA和JamC-QA）上应用五种错误类别（字符转换、字符替换、同音词转换、IME转换和全角转换）评估了十一个日本和多语言模型。

Result: 研究结果表明，字符转换和替换错误始终降低模型的准确性，而IME转换、全角转换和同音词转换对精度影响较小。

Conclusion: 研究结论指出，当前的日语大型语言模型对实际的日语打字错误仍较为脆弱，特别是那些大量扭曲原始输入的打字错误，强调了在实际输入环境中进行鲁棒性评估的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance across various natural language processing tasks. However, their robustness to typographical errors remains underexplored, particularly in Japanese, where text input involves multiple writing systems and IME-based conversion. In this study, we evaluate the robustness of Japanese LLMs against realistic Japanese-specific typos. We introduce five typo categories: Character Transposition, Character Replacement, Homophone Conversion, Japanese IME Conversion, and Full-Width Conversion. These perturbations are applied to three Japanese benchmark datasets (JMMLU, JCommonsenseQA, and JamC-QA), and eleven Japanese and multilingual LLMs are evaluated. The results show that Character Transposition and Character Replacement typos consistently reduce accuracy across benchmarks, whereas IME Conversion, Full-Width Conversion, and Homophone Conversion have relatively limited impact. These findings reveal that current Japanese LLMs remain vulnerable to realistic Japanese typing errors, particularly those that substantially distort the original input, highlighting the importance of robustness evaluation in practical input environments.

</details>


### [144] [Right Answers, Wrong States: Hidden Information Failures in Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01244)
*Herun Wan,Jiaying Wu,Minnan Luo,Zihan Ma,Fanxiao Li,Nancy F. Chen,Min-Yen Kan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 当前协作模型在处理多轮次任务时，虽然能够完成任务，但可能会留下不可靠的信息状态。研究引入了OffQuery框架来评估证据验证、共享状态重建和任务解决这三个方面的能力，并提出ReGround方法来提高协作系统的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多代理系统评估主要关注是否得出正确的答案，忽视了合作过程中可能产生的信息腐化问题。作者通过引入OffQuery框架，分析了在高代价场景（如医疗和灾难响应）中多代理系统的合作表现，特别是在证据验证、共享状态重建和任务解决中的表现差异。

Method: 作者首先使用GPT、Gemini和Qwen等模型，在医疗和灾害响应两个高代价场景中，分别评估了协作模型在这三个方面的表现。接着揭示了协作模型在可靠信息状态重建上的不足，并提出了ReGround方法来解决这个问题。

Result: 研究发现，标准协作模型在任务解决方面表现较好，但在证据验证和共享状态重建方面表现较差。ReGround方法在七个模型中提高了这三个方面的表现，平均提升比例分别为309.0%、82.9%和17.6%。

Conclusion: 可靠的协作不仅需要正确完成当前任务，还需要保持一个可靠的共享状态以供后续任务推论。ReGround方法提高了协作模型在三个关键方面的表现，表明了通过确保信息一致性可以显著提高多代理系统的可靠性。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems are often judged by whether they reach the correct answer. This can miss a distinct failure: collaboration may leave behind a corrupted information state even when the immediate decision is correct. We call this an off-query failure. To study this failure in collaborative decision support, we introduce OffQuery, which separately evaluates evidence verification (T1), shared-state reconstruction (T2), and task resolution (T3) in two representative high-stakes settings: healthcare and disaster response. Across GPT, Gemini, and Qwen models, standard collaboration shows much stronger task performance than state reliability. Averaged over 21 model--setting combinations, task resolution reaches 64.7%, while evidence verification and state reconstruction reach only 14.3% and 43.1%. We trace this gap to selective information use: current queries often bypass corrupted facts, which become consequential when later tasks require them. We further introduce ReGround, which resolves conflicting evidence, verifies shared facts, reconstructs a trusted state, and reasons over that state. Across seven models from three families, ReGround improves all three capabilities in every evaluated setting, with average relative gains of 309.0%, 82.9%, and 17.6% on T1, T2, and T3. Reliable collaboration therefore requires both a correct decision and a reliable shared state for future reasoning.

</details>


### [145] [Science Utopia? Closed-Loop LLM Simulation of Academic Research Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01257)
*Yiqiao Jin,Yiyang Wang,Lucheng Fu,Bing He,Siheng Xiong,Yijia Xiao,B. Aditya Prakash,Josiah Hester,Srijan Kumar,James Evans,Jindong Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SciUtopia是一款持久的封闭回路LLM代理仿真框架，用于研究学术研究生态系统。它模拟了包括研究方向选择、合作、提交、同行评审、重提交、引用、资助和研究人员流失在内的相互关联的科研过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能在科研全周期中的作用越来越大，理解这些相互连接和发展的过程变得越来越重要。本文介绍的SciUtopia为研究学术研究生态系统提供了一个受控的实验平台，可以进行匹配的反事实实验和针对性干预。

Method: SciUtopia通过模拟多种科研过程，包括研究方向选择、合作、提交、同行评审、重提交、引用、资助和研究人员流失，来研究学术研究生态系统。这些过程在模拟跨年度中保持演化。

Result: SciUtopia在61个模拟世界中模拟了超过40000名研究人员和8000个机构，产生了大约40万次研究决定和120万次LLM生成的同行评审。

Conclusion: 研究发现，基于拒绝的重提交显著增加了审稿负担；谨慎探索在平衡引文影响和职业生涯成功以及长期主题多样性之间取得平衡；资源分配不平等甚至在没有明显累积优势的早期资金中也会出现。

Abstract: Scientific progress emerges from a longitudinal ecosystem in which researchers, institutions, funding agencies, collaboration networks, and the scientific literature co-evolve. As AI becomes increasingly involved throughout the scientific research cycle, understanding these interconnected and evolving processes becomes increasingly important. We introduce SciUtopia, a persistent, closed-loop LLM-agent simulation framework for studying academic research ecosystems. SciUtopia models interconnected scientific processes such as research-direction choice, collaboration, submission, peer review, resubmission, citation, funding, and researcher attrition, while maintaining evolving states across simulated years. Its configurable institutional mechanisms and information channels provide a controlled testbed for matched counterfactual experiments and targeted interventions. Across 61 simulation worlds, SciUtopia simulates over 40,000 researchers from 8,000 institutions, producing around 400,000 publication decisions and 1.2 million LLM-generated peer reviews. Using these longitudinal simulations, we find that rejection-driven resubmission substantially amplifies reviewer burden beyond population growth alone, cautious exploration balances citation impact with career success and long-term topic diversity, and resource inequality can emerge even without detectable cumulative advantage from narrowly winning early funding. Code is available at https://github.com/Ahren09/ScienceUtopia.

</details>


### [146] [Know When to Hold 'em: Correct-Token Retention in Uniform-State Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01275)
*Mojtaba Nafez,James Henderson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究发现当前的一致状态扩散模型在自我纠正过程中存在过度编辑和不协调的问题，这会导致样本多样性降低。提出了一种无损令牌保留正则化（CTR-Reg）方法，该方法可以显著提高无损令牌的准确性，同时保持受损令牌的准确性几乎不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的一致状态扩散模型（USDMs）虽然能够在任何步骤纠正任何标记，但存在的问题是这些模型在自我纠正过程中过于频繁地修正标记，导致样本多样性降低。

Method: 该方法提出了一个简单的辅助损失函数——正确令牌保留正则化（CTR-Reg）来解决这一问题。CTR-Reg旨在训练模型保留未被前向过程扰动的标记。

Result: CTR-Reg在六项基准测试中平均提高了无损令牌准确性26.5个百分点，而受损令牌的准确性几乎没有变化。在使用五步贪婪尾部推理时，生成困惑度在三种模型中下降了一半以上，同时保持了多样性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，正确的标记保留是自纠正扩散语言模型的关键缺失成分，CTR-Reg方法为这一缺口提供了一个有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Uniform-state diffusion models (USDMs) can revise any token at any denoising step, which lets them correct their own mistakes, a key advantage over masked diffusion. Self-correction, however, requires both revising incorrect tokens and retaining correct ones, and we show that current USDMs lack the latter. Even under greedy-tail decoding, state-of-the-art USDMs (DUO, UDLM, and uniform-noise SEDD) keep revising 173--270 of 512 positions at every step, and these large, uncoordinated edits collapse sample diversity. A random-token corruption experiment traces this deficit to the models themselves: they reconstruct clean and corrupted tokens with nearly identical accuracy, even though clean tokens are easier targets. A decomposition of the validation NELBO shows that training barely rewards retention: incorrect predictions are heavily penalized at corrupted positions but almost free at clean ones. We propose Correct-Token Retention Regularization (CTR-Reg), a simple but effective auxiliary loss that trains the model to retain tokens left unperturbed by the forward process and requires no change to the sampler.
